# T4 — working without internet, and in every language

**Territory:** `public/sw.js` · `public/offline.html` · `public/panels/swreg.js` ·
`public/panels/offline.js` · `components/OfflineNotice.tsx` · `components/OfflineShell.tsx` ·
`components/ConnectionBadge.tsx` · `components/AppShell.tsx` · `components/BanGate.tsx` ·
`components/BotTrap.tsx` · `components/Maintenance.tsx` · `lib/i18n.ts`

**Phase block:** P01501–P02000 (500, permanent, never renumbered)
**Branch:** `sweep6/t4-guest-offline-sw-i18n` · **worktree:** `sweep6/T4` · **port:** 4104

## How the 500 are budgeted

| band | ids | count | what it is |
|------|-----|-------|------------|
| A | P01501–P01700 | 200 (40%) | reading the code for correctness |
| B | P01701–P01800 | 100 (20%) | conformance to the project rules named in the prompt |
| C | P01801–P01875 | 75 (15%) | watching it run (headless Playwright, own port 4104) |
| D | P01876–P01925 | 50 (10%) | screenshots actually opened and looked at (1280×800 and A35 360×780 dpr3) |
| E | P01926–P01975 | 50 (10%) | tracing one change across panels |
| F | P01976–P02000 | 25 (5%) | judgment: does a real restaurant need it to work this way |

Per-file coverage: every owned file has at least one row; the six files named in the scope
paragraph (`sw.js`, `offline.html`, `swreg.js`, `panels/offline.js`, `OfflineNotice.tsx`,
`i18n.ts`) have ten or more.

---

## 🔁 SWEEP #7 RE-RUN — 2026-08-22/23, against `origin/main` b64951ad

**Every one of the 500 rows below (P01501–P02000) was re-executed, not sampled.** 300 of them are
code-reading claims and were re-checked by script, one assertion per row; the live rows were driven
against a production build on **port 4204** (my own port, proved mine before anything was trusted);
`npm run verify:offline` was re-run in full — **55 passed, 0 failed**, and it tidied up tables 2, 3
and 28 after itself.

**No regression.** Every row that was `✅` is still `✅`. Every row that sweep #6 marked `❌→✅` is
still green.

**Ten of my own re-run assertions came back red and every single one was MY DETECTOR, not the
code** — verified by reading each line. They are recorded here because a withdrawn finding is as
useful as a confirmed one: it stops the next sweep re-filing it. The best of them is a lesson in
itself: my comment-stripper read `/api/editor/*` inside a `//` comment as the start of a block
comment and silently swallowed the whole `NEVER` list, which is this repo's own recorded
"half a comment eats the next rule" trap, reproduced inside the checker.

**Four rows moved, and one was a genuine fault in the record:**

| row | what changed |
|---|---|
| **P01936** | **The only ❌ of the re-run, and it was FALSE WHEN FILED.** It claimed `LFH_PING`/`LFH_PONG` is "used by `verify-warm-shell.mjs`" — `git log -S LFH_PING --all` shows the string has only ever appeared in `public/sw.js`. Nothing called it. **Fixed (item 4):** `verify:warm-shell` now uses it to ask the running worker its VERSION, which also puts the first guard on the VERSION↔cache-name contract. |
| P01603 | Expectation MOVED, legitimately: the `#home` button is route-aware since v10, so it no longer just sets `location.href = "/"`. Re-checked against the new behaviour. |
| P01669 | Expectation refined. `changesWord()` covers the four TITLES; the offline branch spells its plural inline in the SUB. Identical words, so not a defect — see P17090. |
| P01939 | Expectation refined. `PanelFrame` injects `--safe-t`; each panel's own stylesheet derives `--sat` from it. The substance holds. |

**New checks this run: `P16601`–`P17100` (500).** 497 green, 3 were real problems and are fixed on
this branch. Three findings in total, plus one carried in from a false ledger claim — all four in
`.claude/sweep/T4-findings.md`, one commit each.

Result key: ✅ pass · ❌ real problem (written to `T4-findings.md`, then fixed) · ⏭ skipped with a
written reason.

## Band C — watching it run, headless on port 4104 (P01801–P01875)

All rows in this band were run against a **production build** started on port 4104 from the T4
worktree (`npm run build && npx next start --port 4104`), driven by Playwright imported from that
worktree's own `node_modules`. `next dev` cannot answer these questions — it serves chunks
`no-cache`, so an offline reload can never hydrate.

| id | check | how to verify | result | note |
|----|-------|---------------|--------|------|

Planned by reading my own territory first, then written. The mix, and why each band exists:

| band | ids | n | what it is |
|---|---|---|---|
| G | P16601–P16627 | 27 | **the v10/v11 route-aware "way out"** — brand-new ground the old ledger predates. Driven live and CLICKED, every guest door and the staff door |
| H | P16628–P16759 | 132 | **the dictionary's WORDS, not its keys** — `verify:i18n-scope` already proves every key exists; this asks whether each VALUE is right, per key per language |
| I/J | P16760–P16888 | 129 | **sw.js and the offline bar, second pass** — the worker's own regex lists and `barState` are EXTRACTED AND RUN, so every sentence a person can read is checked as a rendered string |
| K | P16889–P16940 | 52 | **live, offline, on a phone** — all the guest doors, the strip's wording and geometry, the last-resort page at both sizes |
| L | P16941–P16971 | 31 | **all six languages RENDERED and looked at** — glyph shaping is invisible to a text assertion |
| M | P16972–P17086 | 115 | **my own guards' honesty**, the components sweep #6 gave least attention (AppShell, BanGate, BotTrap, Maintenance, OfflineShell), and the judgment questions |
| N | P17087–P17100 | 14 | **the loose ends I noticed while reading** — each judged as defect or not, and recorded either way |

**497 ✅ · 3 ❌→✅ (fixed on this branch) · 0 left failing.**

things that LOOK like faults and are not — three dead `NEVER` entries, an inline plural, an
untracked one-shot timer, dead CSS in a template literal. They are here so the eighth sweep does
not file them.

# ⚠️ SWEEP #8 · TERMINAL 4 of 40 — THE TABLE SESSION, AND WHO THIS DEVICE IS
## phases P57701–P58204 (504 rows) · block P57701–P58700, PRE-ALLOCATED, never claimed from the index
**Sweep #8 re-cut the territories from the real file structure, so this file now holds TWO
terminals' work.** Everything ABOVE this banner is sweep #6's terminal 4 (the guest offline layer,
the service worker and i18n) — untouched here except for eight rows naming a file T4 owns in sweep
#8, whose `result` columns carry a `· s8` re-run mark. Everything BELOW is sweep #8's terminal 4.
**Territory (write access):** `components/SessionGate.tsx` · `components/SessionStatusWidget.tsx` ·
`lib/session.ts` · `app/pair/page.tsx` · `app/api/pair/route.ts` · `app/api/rt-config/route.ts` ·
`app/api/r/[restaurant]/menu-data/route.ts`. Re-derived from the filesystem, not trusted from the
roadmap.
**Branch** `sweep8/t4-table-session-and-who-you-are` · **worktree** `~/Documents/Projects/wt-s8-t4` ·
**dev server port 4304** (proved free with `lsof -nP -iTCP:4304 -sTCP:LISTEN` before it was started;
port 4000 was never touched). **Restaurant driven:** My Little French House. **Aangan was never
written to. AV live was never read, named or touched. Not one row was written to the database this
run** — every live check is a read, a rendered assertion, or an event dispatched inside the page.
## Stage 0 — the existing rows, re-run FIRST
109 rows across ten ledger files name a file this territory owns. **All 109 were re-run and their
`result` updated in place, in the file where they live: 108 ✅, 1 ⏭, and ZERO regressions.** The one
⏭ is T2's `P15932`, a handoff about the dish page and `public/sw.js` — neither is T4's file in sweep
#8, so it was re-read and not re-driven. One row (`P29816`) first read RED and was **my own detector
being wrong**, not a regression: it grepped `lib/menuDataServer.ts` for `.select(`, but that file
builds the bundle through `CARD_COLUMNS` and `toCardItem` in `lib/menu.ts`. Measured on the wire
instead (`P58155`): no staff-only column reaches a diner.
Five more of my own new detectors read RED against correct code and were fixed before filing —
recorded because this ledger's own rule is that a static hit is guilty until read: a step count I
typed from memory; two `setStep` calls hidden inside a ternary; a grep that passed against the very
comment recording a deletion; a case-insensitive `N/A` matching "opeN/Approve"; and a "no fast
timer" rule that flagged a one-shot 50ms wait for a `<video>` element to exist.

**Result key:** ✅ pass · ❌ real problem (fixed in this PR unless the note says otherwise) ·
⏭ not reachable this run, with the reason and the next step written in.
**Ten problems were found and fixed** (report items 1–10 and 12). The four that matter most: the
gate's own **Scan QR could not read a single QR sticker this product prints** — every one is
`/q/<code>` since migration 210 and the reader knew only `?table=N` — and it said nothing while
failing; **closing the sheet mid-send told the basket the order was cancelled** while the kitchen
kept it; the **Allow page told a signed-in person to sign in** when the site could not be reached;
and the messages that end a diner's table **flashed for 1.28 seconds wearing a green tick**
(measured on the rendered page, before and after).
**A note on the ❌ column below: it is empty.** Every one of the ten problems was found while the
rows were being WRITTEN — by reading the file the row was about — so the row was authored against
the fixed code and executed green. The evidence that each fault was real is the measurement in the
report and the guard that now fails without the fix, not a red cell here.

# ⚠️ SWEEP #8 · TERMINAL 4 — ROUND 2 · phases P58205–P58567 (363 rows)

**The owner's instruction, 2026-09-02, after round 1 was merged and deployed:** *"after making it
live and merging plan 500 phases test within your boundaries make sure it cover everthing within
your boundries and test everything again if any error left"*.

**Round 2 is deliberately NOT round 1 again.** Round 1 was 504 rows and 405 of them were reads —
because the territory's hardest ground needs a LIVE session at an OPEN table, and round 1 never
opened one. So this round goes to exactly the places round 1 could only read:

* **a real session, opened and closed by this run** — the head's join, the floor seeing their name,
  the phone's session note, and **the floating table card photographed for the first time in any
  sweep**. Every row it wrote was deleted by its own id in a `finally`.
* **the order actually in flight** — the fix from round 1's item 2, driven: the send held open in
  the browser, the sheet dismissed mid-flight, and the basket proved to hear the send's real result
  rather than the cancellation the close used to invent. Nothing was written: both the session read
  and the order POST were answered inside the page.
* **the Allow door signed in** — as a manager, an owner, a kitchen user, a waiter tablet AND the
  Aevidine admin. Round 1 only ever saw it signed out.
* **the live-socket config for every role**, including that a `?rid=` cannot move a real staff member.
* **the one name across the whole sheet** (round 1's item 14 landed after round 1's ledger was filed).
* **offline and a restaurant that will not answer**, both driven.
* **the encoded regression suite** — all 30 `verify:*` guards that touch this ground, re-run.

**Base:** `origin/main` `90113a16` (round 1 merged, PR #1235). **Ports 4304 (dev) and 4344
(production)**, each proved free AND proved mine by pid before anything on it was trusted — see
`P58550`, which records the one time this round I got that wrong. **French House written to,
Aangan's switches untouched, AV live never read or named.**

**Result: 363 rows, 0 red.** Round 1's twelve fixes are all still in place on merged main, and each
was re-driven rather than re-read.

**On the number:** this is 363 rows, not 500. `P57701`–`P58700` is this terminal's whole
pre-allocated block and round 1 spent 504 of it; what is here is what was left, spent on ground that
had never been driven rather than padded out to a round figure. The sweep rules say plainly that
there is no quota and that a terminal inventing checks to look busy costs more than one reporting
greens.

**What this round still could not reach, named rather than glossed:** a SECOND diner joining a
head's table and being approved or declined. That needs two live devices against one session plus
the head's approve poller, which lives in `components/SessionOwner.tsx` — not a file this territory
owns. The head's half was driven in full.

It refused a DEV server honestly — dev serves its JavaScript no-cache, so a browser may not keep it and every offline check would fail for a reason that is not the app. Learned the hard way this round: my FIRST attempt took port 4305, which was already another session's — my own next start died with EADDRINUSE and the guard ran against THEIR server. That result was discarded and redone. |
